📖 LEARNED: Reading Weather (Level 1). Red sky at night, shepherd's delight. Red sky at morning, shepherd's warning. It works.

📖 LEARNED: Radios & Signals (Level 1). Continuity, batteries, and solder. Most dead electronics are just broken connections.
